function MaterialMon()
    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Radioactive Material' then
        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Mystic Droplet' then
            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Magma Ore' then
                if SelectMaterial ~= 'Angel Wings' then
                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Leather' then
                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Scrap Metal' then
                            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Fish Tail' then
                                if SelectMaterial ~= 'Demonic Wisp' then
                                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Vampire Fang' then
                                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Conjured Cocoa' then
                                            if SelectMaterial ~= 'Dragon Scale' then
                                                if SelectMaterial ~= 'Gunpowder' then
                                                    if SelectMaterial ~= 'Hydra Enforcer' then
                                                        if SelectMaterial ~= 'Venomous Assailant' then
                                                            if SelectMaterial == 'Mini Tusk' then
                                                                MMon = 'Mythological Pirate'
                                                                MPos = CFrame.new()
                                                                SP = 'Default'
                                                            end
                                                        else
                                                            MMon = 'Venomous Assailant'
                                                            MPos = CFrame.new(4879.92041015625, 1089.46142578125, 1104.00830078125)
                                                            SP = 'Default'
                                                        end
                                                    else
                                                        MMon = 'Hydra Enforcer'
                                                        MPos = CFrame.new(4581.517578125, 1001.55908203125, 704.9378662109375)
                                                        SP = 'Default'
                                                    end
                                                else
                                                    MMon = 'Pistol Billionaire'
                                                    MPos = CFrame.new(-469, 74, 5904)
                                                    SP = 'Default'
                                                end
                                            else
                                                MMon = 'Dragon Crew Archer'

                                        end

                                        MMon = 'Vampire'

                                        SP = 'Default'
                                    end
                                else
                                    MMon = 'Demonic Soul'

                                    SP = 'Default'
                                end
                            elseif Sea3 then
                                MMon = 'Fishman Raider'
                                MPos = CFrame.new(-10993, 332, -8940)
                                SP = 'Default'
                            elseif Sea1 then
                                MMon = 'Fishman Warrior'
                                MPos = CFrame.new(61123, 19, 1569)
                                SP = 'Default'

                                if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.342342376708984, 1819.7841796875)).Magnitude >= 17000 then
                                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 5.342342376708984, 1819.7841796875))
                                end
                            end
                        elseif Sea1 then

                        elseif Sea2 then

                            MPos = CFrame.new(878, 122, 1235)
                            SP = 'Default'
                        elseif Sea3 then
                            MMon = 'Jungle Pirate'

function UpdateIslandESP()
    local v54, v55, v56 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local u57

        v56, u57 = v54(v55, v56)

        if v56 == nil then
            break
        end

        pcall(function()
            if IslandESP then
                if u57.Name ~= 'Sea' then
                    if u57:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                        u57.NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = u57.Name .. '   \n' .. round((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u57.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                    else
                        local _BillboardGui = Instance.new('BillboardGui', u57)

                        _BillboardGui.Name = 'NameEsp'
                        _BillboardGui.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        _BillboardGui.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                        _BillboardGui.Adornee = u57
                        _BillboardGui.AlwaysOnTop = true

                        local _TextLabel2 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui)

                        _TextLabel2.Font = 'GothamBold'
                        _TextLabel2.FontSize = 'Size14'
                        _TextLabel2.TextWrapped = true
                        _TextLabel2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        _TextLabel2.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                        _TextLabel2.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        _TextLabel2.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                        _TextLabel2.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 0, 0)
                    end
                end
            elseif u57:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                u57:FindFirstChild('NameEsp'):Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end
function isnil(p60)
    return p60 == nil
end

local function u62(p61)
    return math.floor(tonumber(p61) + 0.5)
end

Number = math.random(1, 1000000)

function UpdatePlayerChams()
    local v63, v64, v65 = pairs(game:GetService('Players'):GetChildren())

    while true do
        local u66

        v65, u66 = v63(v64, v65)

        if v65 == nil then
            break
        end

        pcall(function()
            if not isnil(u66.Character) then
                if ESPPlayer then
                    if isnil(u66.Character.Head) or u66.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                        u66.Character.Head['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = u66.Name .. ' | ' .. u62((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u66.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance\nHealth : ' .. u62(u66.Character.Humanoid.Health * 100 / u66.Character.Humanoid.MaxHealth) .. '%'
                    else
                        local _BillboardGui2 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', u66.Character.Head)

                        _BillboardGui2.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
                        _BillboardGui2.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        _BillboardGui2.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                        _BillboardGui2.Adornee = u66.Character.Head
                        _BillboardGui2.AlwaysOnTop = true

                        local _TextLabel3 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui2)

                        _TextLabel3.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
                        _TextLabel3.FontSize = 'Size10'
                        _TextLabel3.TextWrapped = true
                        _TextLabel3.Text = u66.Name .. ' \n' .. u62((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u66.Character.Head.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                        _TextLabel3.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        _TextLabel3.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                        _TextLabel3.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        _TextLabel3.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5

                        if u66.Team ~= game.Players.LocalPlayer.Team then
                            _TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.new(255, 0, 0)
                        else
                            _TextLabel3.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 254)
                        end
                    end
                elseif u66.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                    u66.Character.Head:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

function Tween2(p216)
    local _Magnitude = (p216.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
    local _ = 350 <= _Magnitude
    local v218 = 350
    local v219 = TweenInfo.new(_Magnitude / v218, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    local v220 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, v219, {CFrame = p216})

    v220:Play()

    if _G.CancelTween2 then
        v220:Cancel()
    end

    _G.Clip2 = true

    wait(_Magnitude / v218)

    _G.Clip2 = false
end
function BTPZ(p221)
    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p221

    task.wait()

    game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p221
end

TweenSpeed = 350

function Tween(p222)
    local _Magnitude2 = (p222.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude
    local v224 = TweenSpeed

    if _Magnitude2 >= 350 then
        v224 = TweenSpeed
    end

    local v225 = TweenInfo.new(_Magnitude2 / v224, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
    local v226 = game:GetService('TweenService'):Create(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart, v225, {CFrame = p222})

    v226:Play()

    if _G.StopTween then
        v226:Cancel()
    end
end
function CancelTween(p227)
    if not p227 then
        _G.StopTween = true

        wait()
        Tween(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
        wait()

        _G.StopTween = false
    end
end
function EquipTool(p228)
    if game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p228) then
        local v229 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild(p228)

        wait()
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v229)
    end
end

spawn(function()
    local v230 = getrawmetatable(game)
    local ___namecall = v230.__namecall

    setreadonly(v230, false)

    v230.__namecall = newcclosure(function(...)
        local v232 = getnamecallmethod()
        local v233 = {...}

        if tostring(v232) ~= 'FireServer' or (tostring(v233[1]) ~= 'RemoteEvent' or (tostring(v233[2]) == 'true' or (tostring(v233[2]) == 'false' or not _G.UseSkill))) then
            return ___namecall(...)
        end
        if type(v233[2]) ~= 'vector' then
            v233[2] = CFrame.new(PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit)
        else
            v233[2] = PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit
        end

        return ___namecall(unpack(v233))
    end)
end)

function CheckMaterial(p239)
    local v240, v241, v242 = pairs(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))

    while true do
        local v243

        v242, v243 = v240(v241, v242)

        if v242 == nil then
            break
        end
        if type(v243) == 'table' and (v243.Type == 'Material' and v243.Name == p239) then
            return v243.Count
        end
    end

    return 0
end
function GetWeaponInventory(p244)
    local v245, v246, v247 = pairs(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('getInventory'))

    while true do
        local v248

        v247, v248 = v245(v246, v247)

        if v247 == nil then
            break
        end
        if type(v248) == 'table' and (v248.Type == 'Sword' and v248.Name == p244) then
            return true
        end
    end

    return false
end

local _LocalPlayer = game.Players.LocalPlayer

function FindEnemiesInRange(p250, p251)
    local _Position = (_LocalPlayer.Character or _LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Wait()):GetPivot().Position
    local v253, v254, v255 = ipairs(p251)
    local v256 = nil

    while true do
        local v257

        v255, v257 = v253(v254, v255)

        if v255 == nil then
            break
        end
        if not v257:GetAttribute('IsBoat') and (v257:FindFirstChildOfClass('Humanoid') and v257.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
            local _Head = v257:FindFirstChild('Head')

            if _Head and ((_Position - _Head.Position).Magnitude <= 60 and v257 ~= _LocalPlayer.Character) then
                table.insert(p250, {v257, _Head})

                v256 = _Head
            end
        end
    end

    local v259, v260, v261 = ipairs(game.Players:GetPlayers())

    while true do
        local v262

        v261, v262 = v259(v260, v261)

        if v261 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v262.Character and v262 ~= _LocalPlayer then
            local _Head2 = v262.Character:FindFirstChild('Head')

            if _Head2 and (_Position - _Head2.Position).Magnitude <= 60 then
                table.insert(p250, {
                    v262.Character,
                    _Head2,
                })

                v256 = _Head2
            end
        end
    end

    return v256
end
function GetEquippedTool()
    local _Character = _LocalPlayer.Character

    if not _Character then
        return nil
    end

    local v265, v266, v267 = ipairs(_Character:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v268

        v267, v268 = v265(v266, v267)

        if v267 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v268:IsA('Tool') then
            return v268
        end
    end

    return nil
end
function AttackNoCoolDown()
    local u269 = {}
    local v270 = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren()
    local u271 = FindEnemiesInRange(u269, v270)

    if u271 then
        if GetEquippedTool() then
            pcall(function()
                local _ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage')
                local _RERegisterAttack = _ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RE/RegisterAttack')
                local _RERegisterHit = _ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild('Modules'):WaitForChild('Net'):WaitForChild('RE/RegisterHit')

                if #u269 <= 0 then
                    task.wait(1e-9)
                else
                    _RERegisterAttack:FireServer(1e-9)
                    _RERegisterHit:FireServer(u271, u269)
                end
            end)
        end
    else
        return
    end
end

Type = 1

spawn(function()
    while wait() do
        if Type ~= 1 then
            if Type ~= 2 then
                if Type ~= 3 then
                    if Type ~= 4 then
                        if Type == 5 then
                            Pos = CFrame.new(0, 40, -40)
                        end
                    else
                        Pos = CFrame.new(0, 40, 40)
                    end
                else
                    Pos = CFrame.new(40, 40, 0)
                end
            else
                Pos = CFrame.new(-40, 40, 0)
            end
        else
            Pos = CFrame.new(0, 40, 0)
        end
    end
end)

end)

        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Buso')
    end
end
function to(p275)
    repeat
        wait(_G.Fast_Delay)
        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:ChangeState(15)

        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p275

        task.wait()

        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p275
    until (p275.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 2000
end
function to(p276)
    pcall(function()
        if (p276.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude >= 2000 and (not Auto_Raid and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
            if NameMon ~= 'FishmanQuest' then
                if Mon ~= "God's Guard" then
                    if NameMon ~= 'SkyExp1Quest' then
                        if NameMon ~= 'ShipQuest1' then
                            if NameMon ~= 'ShipQuest2' then
                                if NameMon ~= 'FrostQuest' then
                                    repeat
                                        wait(_G.Fast_Delay)

                                        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p276

                                        wait(0.05)
                                        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Head:Destroy()

                                        game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = p276
                                    until (p276.Position - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude < 2500 and game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid.Health > 0

                                    wait()
                                else
                                    Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                                    wait()
                                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(-6508.5581054688, 89.034996032715, -132.83953857422))
                                end
                            else
                                Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                                wait()
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(923.21252441406, 126.9760055542, 32852.83203125))
                            end
                        else
                            Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                            wait()
                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(923.21252441406, 126.9760055542, 32852.83203125))
                        end
                    else
                        Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                        wait()
                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(-7894.6176757813, 5547.1416015625, -380.29119873047))
                    end
                else
                    Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                    wait()
                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(-4607.82275, 872.54248, -1667.55688))
                end
            else
                Tween(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame)
                wait()
                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(61163.8515625, 11.6796875, 1819.7841796875))
            end
        end
    end)
end

            local _Position2 = (_LocalPlayer2.Character or _LocalPlayer2.CharacterAdded:Wait()):GetPivot().Position
            local __ChestTagged = game:GetService('CollectionService'):GetTagged('_ChestTagged')
            local _huge = math.huge
            local v325 = nil

            for v326 = 1, #__ChestTagged do
                local v327 = __ChestTagged[v326]
                local _Magnitude3 = (v327:GetPivot().Position - _Position2).Magnitude

                if not v327:GetAttribute('IsDisabled') then
                    if _Magnitude3 < _huge then
                        v325 = v327
                        _huge = _Magnitude3
                    end
                end
            end

            if v325 then
                local _Position3 = v325:GetPivot().Position
                local v330 = CFrame.new(_Position3)

                Tween2(v330)
            end
        end
    end
end)

_SliderHealt:SetValue(20)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        if _G.UseSkill then
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.UseSkill then
                    return
                end

                local v337, v338, v339 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())
                local v340

                v339, v340 = v337(v338, v339)

                if v339 == nil then
                end
                if v340.Name ~= MonFarm or (not v340:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') or (not v340:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') or v340.Humanoid.Health > v340.Humanoid.MaxHealth * KillPercent / 100)) then
                end

                game:GetService('RunService').Heartbeat:wait()
                EquipTool(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.DevilFruit.Value)
                Tween(v340.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit = v340.HumanoidRootPart.Position

                if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.DevilFruit.Value) then
                    game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.DevilFruit.Value).MousePos.Value = PositionSkillMasteryDevilFruit

                    local _Value2 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Data.DevilFruit.Value).Level.Value

                    if SkillZ and 1 <= _Value2 then
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'Z', false, game)
                        wait()
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'Z', false, game)
                    end
                    if SkillX and 2 <= _Value2 then
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'X', false, game)
                        wait()
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'X', false, game)
                    end
                    if SkillC and 3 <= _Value2 then
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'C', false, game)
                        wait()
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'C', false, game)
                    end
                    if SkillV and 4 <= _Value2 then
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'V', false, game)
                        wait()
                        game:service('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'V', false, game)
                    end
                    if SkillF and 5 <= _Value2 then
                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'F', false, game)
                        wait()
                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 'F', false, game)
                    end
                end
                if AutoFarmMasDevilFruit and (_G.UseSkill and v340.Humanoid.Health ~= 0) then
                else
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)
spawn(function()
    while task.wait(0.1) do
        if AutoFarmMasDevilFruit and TypeMastery == 'Near Mobs' then
            pcall(function()
                local v342, v343, v344 = pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetChildren())

                while true do
                    local v345

                    v344, v345 = v342(v343, v344)

                    if v344 == nil then
                        return
                    end
                    if v345.Name and (v345:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v345:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v345:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart').Position).Magnitude <= 5000)) then
                        repeat
                            if true then
                                wait(_G.Fast_Delay)

                                if v345.Humanoid.Health > v345.Humanoid.MaxHealth * KillPercent / 100 then
                                    _G.UseSkill = false

                                    AutoHaki()

                                    bringmob = true

                                    EquipTool(SelectWeapon)
                                    Tween(v345.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                                    v345.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
                                    v345.HumanoidRootPart.Transparency = 1
                                    v345.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
                                    v345.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                                    v345.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                    FarmPos = v345.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                    MonFarm = v345.Name

                                    AttackNoCoolDown()
                                else
                                    _G.UseSkill = true
                                end
                            end
                        until not AutoFarmMasDevilFruit or (not MasteryType == 'Near Mobs' or (not v345.Parent or v345.Humanoid.Health == 0))

                        bringmob = false
                        _G.UseSkill = false
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

                                AttackNoCoolDown()
                                AutoHaki()

                                bringmob = true

                                EquipTool(SelectWeapon)
                                Tween(v398.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                                v398.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
                                v398.HumanoidRootPart.Transparency = 1
                                v398.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
                                v398.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                                v398.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                FarmPos = v398.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                MonFarm = v398.Name
                            until not _G.AutoMaterial or (not v398.Parent or v398.Humanoid.Health <= 0)

                            bringmob = false
                        end
                    end
                else
                    local v399, v400, v401 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.EnemySpawns:GetChildren())

                    while true do
                        local v402

                        v401, v402 = v399(v400, v401)

                        if v401 == nil then
                            break
                        end
                        if string.find(v402.Name, Mon) and (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v402.Position).Magnitude >= 10 then
                            Tween(v402.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

    function UpdateIslandKisuneESP()
        local v405, v406, v407 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())

        while true do
            local u408

            if v407 == nil then
                break

                if KitsuneIslandEsp then
                    if u408.Name == 'Kitsune Island' then
                        if u408:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                            u408.NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = u408.Name .. '   \n' .. u191((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u408.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' M'
                        else
                            local _BillboardGui24 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', u408)

                            _BillboardGui24.Name = 'NameEsp'
                            _BillboardGui24.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                            _BillboardGui24.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)

                            _BillboardGui24.AlwaysOnTop = true

                            local _TextLabel25 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui24)

                            _TextLabel25.Font = 'Code'
                            _TextLabel25.FontSize = 'Size14'
                            _TextLabel25.TextWrapped = true
                            _TextLabel25.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                            _TextLabel25.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                            _TextLabel25.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                            _TextLabel25.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                            _TextLabel25.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)

                    end

    function CheckPirateBoat()
        local v587 = next
        local v588, v589 = game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren()
        local v590 = {
            'PirateGrandBrigade',
            'PirateBrigade',
        }

        while true do
            local v591

            v589, v591 = v587(v588, v589)

            if v589 == nil then
                break
            end
            if table.find(v590, v591.Name) and (v591:FindFirstChild('Health') and v591.Health.Value > 0) then
                return v591
            end
        end
    end

    spawn(function()
        while wait() do
            if _G.Ship then
                pcall(function()
                    if CheckPirateBoat() then
                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 32, false, game)
                        wait(0.5)
                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 32, false, game)

                        local v592 = CheckPirateBoat()

                        repeat
                            wait()
                            spawn(Tween(v592.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -20, 0)), 1)

                            AimBotSkillPosition = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -5, 0)
                            Skillaimbot = true
                            AutoSkill = false
                        until not v592 or (not v592.Parent or (v592.Health.Value <= 0 or not CheckPirateBoat()))

                        Skillaimbot = true
                        AutoSkill = false
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
    end)

if Sea3 then
    v49.Sea:AddSection('Mysterious Island')

    local u622 = v49.Sea:AddParagraph({
        Title = 'Status : ',
        Content = '',
    })

    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                local _MoonTextureId = game:GetService('Lighting').Sky.MoonTextureId

                if _MoonTextureId == 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709149431' then
                    FullMoonStatus = '100%'
                elseif _MoonTextureId == 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709149052' then
                    FullMoonStatus = '75%'
                elseif _MoonTextureId == 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709143733' then
                    FullMoonStatus = '50%'
                elseif _MoonTextureId == 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709150401' then
                    FullMoonStatus = '25%'
                elseif _MoonTextureId == 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=9709149680' then
                    FullMoonStatus = '15%'
                else
                    FullMoonStatus = '0%'
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait() do
            pcall(function()
                if game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('MysticIsland') then
                    MirageStatus = '\u{fffd}\u{fffd}\u{fe0f}'
                else
                    MirageStatus = '\u{fffd}\u{fffd}\u{fe0f}'
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            while wait() do
                u622:SetDesc('Mysterious Island: ' .. MirageStatus .. ' | Full Moon: ' .. FullMoonStatus)
            end
        end)
    end)
    v49.Sea:AddButton({
        Title = 'TP To Highest Point',
        Description = '',
        Callback = function()
            TweenToHighestPoint()
        end,
    })

    function TweenToHighestPoint()
        local v624 = getHighestPoint()

        if v624 then
            Tween2(v624.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 211.88, 0))
        end
    end
    function getHighestPoint()
        if not game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('MysticIsland') then
            return nil
        end

        local v625, v626, v627 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Map.MysticIsland:GetDescendants())

        while true do
            local v628

            v627, v628 = v625(v626, v627)

            if v627 == nil then
                break
            end
            if v628:IsA('MeshPart') and v628.MeshId == 'rbxassetid://6745037796' then
                return v628
            end
        end
    end
end

                            end

                                    end

                                        end

                    end

                                        Tween(u693.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                                        u693.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false

                                        AttackNoCoolDown()

                                        PosMon = u693.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                        MonFarm = u693.Name
                                        bringmob = true
                                    end)
                                until _G.Auto_MusketeerHat == false or (not u693.Parent or u693.Humanoid.Health <= 0) or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Quest.Visible == false

                                bringmob = false
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        bringmob = false

                        Tween(CFrame.new(-13206.452148438, 425.89199829102, -7964.5537109375))
                    end
                else
                    Tween(CFrame.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125))

                    if (Vector3.new(-12443.8671875, 332.40396118164, -7675.4892578125) - game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 30 then
                        wait(1.5)
                        game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('StartQuest', 'CitizenQuest', 1)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

                    Tween(CFrame.new(5439.716796875, 84.420944213867, -6715.1635742188))
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

if Sea2 then
    v49.Main:AddToggle('ToggleEvoRace', {
        Title = 'Auto Evo Race V2',
        Description = '',
        Default = false,
    }):OnChanged(function(p798)
        _G.AutoEvoRace = p798
    end)
    _Options.ToggleEvoRace:SetValue(false)
    spawn(function()
        pcall(function()
            while wait(0.1) do
                if _G.AutoEvoRace and not game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race:FindFirstChild('Evolved') then
                    if game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '1') ~= 0 then
                        if game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '1') ~= 1 then
                            if game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '1') == 2 then
                                game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '3')
                            end
                        else
                            pcall(function()
                                if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Flower 1') or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('Flower 1') then
                                    if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Flower 2') or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('Flower 2') then
                                        if not (game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Flower 3') or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('Flower 3')) then
                                            if game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:FindFirstChild('Zombie') then
                                                local v799, v800, v801 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())

                                                while true do
                                                    local v802

                                                    v801, v802 = v799(v800, v801)

                                                    if v801 == nil then
                                                        break
                                                    end
                                                    if v802.Name == 'Zombie' then
                                                        repeat
                                                            task.wait(_G.Fast_Delay)
                                                            AutoHaki()
                                                            EquipTool(SelectWeapon)
                                                            Tween(v802.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                                                            v802.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                                            v802.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(50, 50, 50)

                                                            AttackNoCoolDown()

                                                            FarmPos = v802.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame
                                                            MonFarm = v802.Name
                                                            bringmob = true
                                                        until game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Flower 3') or (not v802.Parent or (v802.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or _G.AutoEvoRace == false))

                                                        bringmob = false
                                                    end
                                                end
                                            else
                                                Tween(CFrame.new(-5685.9233398438, 48.480125427246, -853.23724365234))
                                            end
                                        end
                                    else
                                        Tween(game:GetService('Workspace').Flower2.CFrame)
                                    end
                                else
                                    Tween(game:GetService('Workspace').Flower1.CFrame)
                                end
                            end)
                        end
                    else
                        Tween(CFrame.new(-2779.83521, 72.9661407, -3574.02002, -0.730484903, 6.390141e-8, -0.68292886, 3.5996322e-8, 1, 5.5066703e-8, 0.68292886, 1.5642466999999998e-8, -0.730484903))

                        if (Vector3.new(-2779.83521, 72.9661407, -3574.02002) - game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude <= 4 then
                            wait(1.3)
                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('Alchemist', '2')
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)
end

    while wait() do
        pcall(function()
            local v808, v809, v810 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v811

                v810, v811 = v808(v809, v810)

                if v810 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if _G.BringMob and (bringmob and (v811.Name == MonFarm and (v811:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and v811.Humanoid.Health > 0))) then
                    if v811.Name ~= 'Factory Staff' then
                        if v811.Name == MonFarm and (v811.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FarmPos.Position).Magnitude <= 1000000000 then
                            v811.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = FarmPos
                            v811.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
                            v811.HumanoidRootPart.Transparency = 1
                            v811.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0
                            v811.Humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0

                            if v811.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
                                v811.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
                            end

                            v811.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                            v811.Head.CanCollide = false

                            v811.Humanoid:ChangeState(11)
                            v811.Humanoid:ChangeState(14)
                            sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                        end
                    elseif (v811.HumanoidRootPart.Position - FarmPos.Position).Magnitude <= 1000000000 then
                        v811.Head.CanCollide = false
                        v811.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                        v811.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
                        v811.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame = FarmPos

                        if v811.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then
                            v811.Humanoid.Animator:Destroy()
                        end

                        sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

local u823 = v49.Status:AddParagraph({
    Title = 'Time',
    Content = '',
})

local function u833()
    local _t = os.date('*t')
    local v825 = _t.hour % 24
    local v826 = v825 < 12 and 'AM' or 'PM'
    local __02i02i02is = string.format('%02i:%02i:%02i %s', (v825 - 1) % 12 + 1, _t.min, _t.sec, v826)
    local __02d02d04d = string.format('%02d/%02d/%04d', _t.day, _t.month, _t.year)
    local _LocalizationService = game:GetService('LocalizationService')
    local _LocalPlayer3 = game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer
    local _ = _LocalPlayer3.Name
    local v831, v832 = pcall(function()
        return _LocalizationService:GetCountryRegionForPlayerAsync(_LocalPlayer3)
    end)

    u823:SetDesc(__02d02d04d .. '-' .. __02i02i02is .. ' [ ' .. (not v831 and 'Unknown' or v832) .. ' ]')
end

spawn(function()
    while true do
        u833()
        game:GetService('RunService').RenderStepped:Wait()
    end
end)

local u834 = v49.Status:AddParagraph({
    Title = 'Server Time',
    Content = '',
})

local function u839()
    local v835 = math.floor(workspace.DistributedGameTime + 0.5)
    local v836 = math.floor(v835 / 3600) % 24
    local v837 = math.floor(v835 / 60) % 60
    local v838 = v835 % 60

    u834:SetDesc(string.format('%02d Hours-%02d Minutes-%02d Seconds', v836, v837, v838))
end

spawn(function()
    while task.wait() do
        pcall(u839)
    end
end)

            pcall(function()
                if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Level.Value >= 700 and World1 then
                    if game:GetService('Workspace').Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide ~= false or game:GetService('Workspace').Map.Ice.Door.Transparency ~= 1 then
                        if game:GetService('Workspace').Map.Ice.Door.CanCollide == false and game:GetService('Workspace').Map.Ice.Door.Transparency == 1 then

                                while true do
                                    local v873

                                    v872, v873 = v870(v871, v872)

                                    if v872 == nil then
                                        break
                                    end
                                    if v873.Name == 'Ice Admiral' then
                                        if not v873.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
                                            game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('TravelDressrosa')
                                        elseif v873:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v873:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and v873.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                            OldCFrameSecond = v873.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame

                                            repeat
                                                task.wait(_G.Fast_Delay)
                                                AutoHaki()
                                                EquipTool(SelectWeapon)

                                                v873.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false

        local v887 = require(game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Util.Sound)
        local v888 = game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Util.Sound.Storage.Other:FindFirstChild('LevelUp_Proxy') or game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Util.Sound.Storage.Other:FindFirstChild('LevelUp')

        function v129(p889)
            repeat
                local v890

                p889, v890 = string.gsub(p889, '^(-?%d+)(%d%d%d)', '%1,%2')
            until v890 == 0

            return p889
        end

        v883.new('<Color=Yellow>QUEST COMPLETED!<Color=/>'):Display()
        v883.new('Earned<Color=Yellow>9,999,999,999,999 Exp.<Color=/>(+None)'):Display()
        v883.new('Earned<Color=Green>$9,999,999,999,999<Color=/>'):Display()

        _LocalPlayer4.Data.Exp.Value = 999999999999
        _LocalPlayer4.Data.Beli.Value = _LocalPlayer4.Data.Beli.Value + 999999999999
        delay = 0
        count = 0

        while _LocalPlayer4.Data.Exp.Value - v885(_Data.Level.Value) > 0 do
            _LocalPlayer4.Data.Exp.Value = _LocalPlayer4.Data.Exp.Value - v885(_Data.Level.Value)
            _LocalPlayer4.Data.Level.Value = _LocalPlayer4.Data.Level.Value + 1
            _LocalPlayer4.Data.Points.Value = _LocalPlayer4.Data.Points.Value + 3

            v886({_LocalPlayer4})
            v887:Play(v888.Value)
            v883.new('<Color=Green>LEVEL UP!<Color=/>(' .. _LocalPlayer4.Data.Level.Value .. ')'):Display()

            count = count + 1

            if count >= 5 then
                delay = tick()
                count = 0

                wait()
            end
        end
    end,
})

local _GetFruits = game.ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild('Remotes').CommF_:InvokeServer('GetFruits')

Table_DevilFruitSniper = {}
ShopDevilSell = {}

local v896 = next
local v897 = nil

while true do
    local v898

    v897, v898 = v896(_GetFruits, v897)

    if v897 == nil then
        break
    end

    table.insert(Table_DevilFruitSniper, v898.Name)

    if v898.OnSale then
        table.insert(ShopDevilSell, v898.Name)
    end
end

function UpdateRealFruitEsp()
    local v923, v924, v925 = pairs(game.Workspace.AppleSpawner:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v926

        v925, v926 = v923(v924, v925)

        if v925 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v926:IsA('Tool') then
            if RealFruitEsp then
                if v926.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                    v926.Handle['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v926.Name .. ' ' .. u851((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v926.Handle.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                else
                    local _BillboardGui25 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v926.Handle)

                    _BillboardGui25.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
                    _BillboardGui25.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                    _BillboardGui25.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                    _BillboardGui25.Adornee = v926.Handle
                    _BillboardGui25.AlwaysOnTop = true

                    local _TextLabel26 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui25)

                    _TextLabel26.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
                    _TextLabel26.FontSize = 'Size14'
                    _TextLabel26.TextWrapped = true
                    _TextLabel26.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    _TextLabel26.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                    _TextLabel26.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    _TextLabel26.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                    _TextLabel26.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                    _TextLabel26.Text = v926.Name .. ' \n' .. u851((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v926.Handle.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                end
            elseif v926.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                v926.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local v929, v930, v931 = pairs(game.Workspace.PineappleSpawner:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v932

        v931, v932 = v929(v930, v931)

        if v931 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v932:IsA('Tool') then
            if RealFruitEsp then
                if v932.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                    v932.Handle['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v932.Name .. ' ' .. u851((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v932.Handle.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                else
                    local _BillboardGui26 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v932.Handle)

                    _BillboardGui26.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number
                    _BillboardGui26.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                    _BillboardGui26.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                    _BillboardGui26.Adornee = v932.Handle
                    _BillboardGui26.AlwaysOnTop = true

                    local _TextLabel27 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui26)

                    _TextLabel27.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
                    _TextLabel27.FontSize = 'Size14'
                    _TextLabel27.TextWrapped = true
                    _TextLabel27.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                    _TextLabel27.TextYAlignment = 'Top'
                    _TextLabel27.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    _TextLabel27.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                    _TextLabel27.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 174, 0)
                    _TextLabel27.Text = v932.Name .. ' \n' .. u851((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v932.Handle.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                end
            elseif v932.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                v932.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number):Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local v935, v936, v937 = pairs(game.Workspace.BananaSpawner:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v938

        v937, v938 = v935(v936, v937)

        if v937 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v938:IsA('Tool') then
            if RealFruitEsp then
                if v938.Handle:FindFirstChild('NameEsp' .. Number) then
                    v938.Handle['NameEsp' .. Number].TextLabel.Text = v938.Name .. ' ' .. u851((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - v938.Handle.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' Distance'
                else
                    local _BillboardGui27 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', v938.Handle)

                    _BillboardGui27.Name = 'NameEsp' .. Number

                    _BillboardGui27.Adornee = v938.Handle
                    _BillboardGui27.AlwaysOnTop = true

                    local _TextLabel28 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui27)

                    _TextLabel28.Font = Enum.Font.GothamSemibold

    IslandMirageEsp = p941

    while IslandMirageEsp do
        wait()
        UpdateIslandMirageEsp()
    end

_Options.ToggleIslandMirageEsp:SetValue(false)

function isnil(p942)
    return p942 == nil
end

local function u944(p943)
    return math.floor(tonumber(p943) + 0.5)
end

Number = math.random(1, 1000000)

function UpdateIslandMirageEsp()
    local v945, v946, v947 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local u948

        v947, u948 = v945(v946, v947)

        if v947 == nil then
            break
        end

        pcall(function()
            if MirageIslandESP then
                if u948.Name == 'Mirage Island' then
                    if u948:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                        u948.NameEsp.TextLabel.Text = u948.Name .. '   \n' .. u944((game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character.Head.Position - u948.Position).Magnitude / 3) .. ' M'
                    else
                        local _BillboardGui28 = Instance.new('BillboardGui', u948)

                        _BillboardGui28.Name = 'NameEsp'
                        _BillboardGui28.ExtentsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
                        _BillboardGui28.Size = UDim2.new(1, 200, 1, 30)
                        _BillboardGui28.Adornee = u948
                        _BillboardGui28.AlwaysOnTop = true

                        local _TextLabel29 = Instance.new('TextLabel', _BillboardGui28)

                        _TextLabel29.Font = Enum.Font.Code
                        _TextLabel29.FontSize = Enum.FontSize.Size14
                        _TextLabel29.TextWrapped = true
                        _TextLabel29.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                        _TextLabel29.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
                        _TextLabel29.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                        _TextLabel29.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
                        _TextLabel29.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 245, 245)
                    end
                end
            elseif u948:FindFirstChild('NameEsp') then
                u948:FindFirstChild('NameEsp'):Destroy()
            end
        end)
    end
end

            if _G.Auto_StartRaid and (game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.PlayerGui.Main.Timer.Visible == false and (not game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 1') and (game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip') or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('Special Microchip')))) then
                if Sea2 then
                    Tween2(CFrame.new(-6438.73535, 250.645355, -4501.50684))
                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack({
                        'SetSpawnPoint',
                    }))
                    fireclickdetector(game:GetService('Workspace').Map.CircleIsland.RaidSummon2.Button.Main.ClickDetector)
                elseif Sea3 then
                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer('requestEntrance', Vector3.new(-5075.50927734375, 314.5155029296875, -3150.0224609375))
                    Tween2(CFrame.new(-5017.40869, 314.844055, -2823.0127, -0.925743818, 4.482175e-8, -0.378151238, 4.5550315e-9, 1, 1.0737756e-7, 0.378151238, 9.768162e-8, -0.925743818))
                    game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage').Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer(unpack({
                        'SetSpawnPoint',
                    }))
                    fireclickdetector(game:GetService('Workspace').Map['Boat Castle'].RaidSummon2.Button.Main.ClickDetector)
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

_Options.ToggleNextIsland:SetValue(false)
spawn(function()
    local u957 = {}

    while task.wait() do
        if AutoNextIsland then
            pcall(function()
                local _Character8 = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character

                if _Character8 and _Character8:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') then
                    local _Locations = game:GetService('Workspace')._WorldOrigin.Locations
                    local _Position8 = _Character8.HumanoidRootPart.Position

                    if (_Position8 - Vector3.new(-6438.73535, 250.645355, -4501.50684)).Magnitude < 1 or (_Position8 - Vector3.new(-5017.40869, 314.844055, -2823.0127)).Magnitude < 1 then
                        u957 = {}
                    end
                    if _Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 1') then
                        _G.AutoNear = true
                    end
                    if _Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 2') and not u957['Island 2'] then
                        Tween(_Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 2').CFrame)

                        u957['Island 2'] = true
                        AutoNextIsland = false

                        wait()

                        AutoNextIsland = true
                    elseif _Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 3') and not u957['Island 3'] then
                        Tween(_Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 3').CFrame)

                        u957['Island 3'] = true
                        AutoNextIsland = false

                        wait()

                        AutoNextIsland = true
                    elseif _Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 4') and not u957['Island 4'] then
                        Tween(_Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 4').CFrame)

                        u957['Island 4'] = true
                        AutoNextIsland = false

                        wait()

                        AutoNextIsland = true
                    elseif _Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 5') and not u957['Island 5'] then
                        Tween(_Locations:FindFirstChild('Island 5').CFrame)

                        u957['Island 5'] = true
                        AutoNextIsland = false

                        wait()

                        AutoNextIsland = true
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end)

spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        while wait() do
            if Auto_Law then
                if not game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:FindFirstChild('Order') and (not game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):FindFirstChild('Order') and (game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild('Microchip') or game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild('Microchip'))) then
                    fireclickdetector(game:GetService('Workspace').Map.CircleIsland.RaidSummon.Button.Main.ClickDetector)
                end
                if game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):FindFirstChild('Order') or game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:FindFirstChild('Order') then
                    if game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:FindFirstChild('Order') then
                        local v964, v965, v966 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').Enemies:GetChildren())

                        while true do
                            local v967

                            v966, v967 = v964(v965, v966)

                            if v966 == nil then
                                break
                            end
                            if v967.Name == 'Order' then
                                repeat
                                    wait(_G.Fast_Delay)
                                    AttackNoCoolDown()
                                    AutoHaki()
                                    EquipTool(SelectWeapon)
                                    Tween(v967.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * Pos)

                                    v967.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                                    v967.HumanoidRootPart.Size = Vector3.new(60, 60, 60)
                                until not v967.Parent or (v967.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or Auto_Law == false)
                            end
                        end
                    elseif game:GetService('ReplicatedStorage'):FindFirstChild('Order') then
                        Tween(CFrame.new(-6217.2021484375, 28.047645568848, -5053.1357421875))
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
end)

_Options.ToggleAutotrial:SetValue(false)
spawn(function()
    pcall(function()
        while wait() do
            if _G.AutoQuestRace then
                if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= 'Human' then
                    if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= 'Skypiea' then
                        if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= 'Fishman' then
                            if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= 'Cyborg' then
                                if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value ~= 'Ghoul' then
                                    if game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer.Data.Race.Value == 'Mink' then
                                        local v970, v971, v972 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace'):GetDescendants())

                                        while true do
                                            local v973

                                            v972, v973 = v970(v971, v972)

                                            if v972 == nil then
                                                break
                                            end
                                            if v973.Name == 'StartPoint' then
                                                Tween(v973.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 10, 0))
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                else
                                    local v974, v975, v976 = pairs(game.Workspace.Enemies:GetDescendants())

                                    while true do
                                        local u977

                                        v976, u977 = v974(v975, v976)

                                        if v976 == nil then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        if u977:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (u977:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and u977.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                                            pcall(function()
                                                repeat
                                                    wait()

                                                    u977.Humanoid.Health = 0
                                                    u977.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false

                                                    sethiddenproperty(game.Players.LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)
                                                until not _G.AutoQuestRace or (not u977.Parent or u977.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
                                            end)
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            else
                                Tween(CFrame.new(28654, 14898.7832, -30, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 1))
                            end
                        else
                            local v978, v979, v980 = pairs(game:GetService('Workspace').SeaBeasts.SeaBeast1:GetDescendants())

                            while true do
                                local v981

                                v980, v981 = v978(v979, v980)

                                if v980 == nil then
                                    break
                                end
                                if v981.Name == 'HumanoidRootPart' then
                                    Tween(v981.CFrame * Pos)

                                    local v982, v983, v984 = pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren())

                                    while true do
                                        local v985

                                        v984, v985 = v982(v983, v984)

                                        if v984 == nil then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        if v985:IsA('Tool') and v985.ToolTip == 'Melee' then
                                            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v985)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 122, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 122, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    wait(0.2)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 120, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 120, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    wait(0.2)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 99, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 99, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)

                                    local v986, v987, v988 = pairs(game.Players.LocalPlayer.Backpack:GetChildren())

                                    while true do
                                        local v989

                                        v988, v989 = v986(v987, v988)

                                        if v988 == nil then
                                            break
                                        end
                                        if v989:IsA('Tool') and v989.ToolTip == 'Blox Fruit' then
                                            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v989)
                                        end
                                    end

                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 122, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 122, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    wait(0.2)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 120, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 120, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    wait(0.2)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 99, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 99, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)
                                    wait()

                                        local v993

                                        v992, v993 = v990(v991, v992)

                                        if v992 == nil then
                                            break
                                        end

                                            game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.Humanoid:EquipTool(v993)

                                    end

                                    game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(false, 122, false, game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart)

                        break

                        until not _G.AutoKillTrial or (not v1010.Parent or v1010.Character.Humanoid.Health <= 0)

    end,

function Hop()
    local _PlaceId3 = game.PlaceId
    local u1020 = {}
    local u1021 = ''
    local _hour = os.date('!*t').hour

    function TPReturner()
        local v1023

        if u1021 ~= '' then
            v1023 = game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet('https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/' .. _PlaceId3 .. '/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100&cursor=' .. u1021))
        else
            v1023 = game.HttpService:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet('https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/' .. _PlaceId3 .. '/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100'))
        end
        if v1023.nextPageCursor and (v1023.nextPageCursor ~= 'null' and v1023.nextPageCursor ~= nil) then
            u1021 = v1023.nextPageCursor
        end

        local v1024, v1025, v1026 = pairs(v1023.data)
        local v1027 = 0

        while true do
            local v1028

            v1026, v1028 = v1024(v1025, v1026)

            if v1026 == nil then
                break
            end

            local v1029 = true
            local u1030 = tostring(v1028.id)

            if tonumber(v1028.maxPlayers) > tonumber(v1028.playing) then
                local v1031, v1032, v1033 = pairs(u1020)

                while true do
                    local v1034

                    v1033, v1034 = v1031(v1032, v1033)

                    if v1033 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1027 == 0 then
                        if tonumber(_hour) ~= tonumber(v1034) then
                            pcall(function()
                                u1020 = {}

                                table.insert(u1020, _hour)
                            end)
                        end
                    elseif u1030 == tostring(v1034) then
                        v1029 = false
                    end

                    v1027 = v1027 + 1
                end

                if v1029 == true then
                    table.insert(u1020, u1030)
                    wait()
                    pcall(function()
                        wait()
                        game:GetService('TeleportService'):TeleportToPlaceInstance(_PlaceId3, u1030, game.Players.LocalPlayer)
                    end)
                    wait()
                end
            end
        end
    end

    _World = function()
        while wait() do
            pcall(function()
                TPReturner()

                if u1021 ~= '' then
                    TPReturner()
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    _World()
end

local function u1044()
    local _Lighting = game:GetService('Lighting')

    if _Lighting:FindFirstChild('BaseAtmosphere') then
        _Lighting.BaseAtmosphere:Destroy()
    end
    if _Lighting:FindFirstChild('SeaTerrorCC') then
        _Lighting.SeaTerrorCC:Destroy()
    end
    if _Lighting:FindFirstChild('LightingLayers') then
        if _Lighting.LightingLayers:FindFirstChild('Atmosphere') then
            _Lighting.LightingLayers.Atmosphere:Destroy()
        end

        wait()

        if _Lighting.LightingLayers:FindFirstChild('DarkFog') then
            _Lighting.LightingLayers.DarkFog:Destroy()
        end
    end

    _Lighting.FogEnd = 100000
end

local function u1080(p1069)
    local _LocalPlayer6 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local _Backpack = _LocalPlayer6.Backpack
    local v1072, v1073, v1074 = pairs(_Backpack:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v1075

        v1074, v1075 = v1072(v1073, v1074)

        if v1074 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v1075:IsA('Tool') and v1075.ToolTip == p1069 then
            v1075.Parent = _LocalPlayer6.Character

            local v1076, v1077, v1078 = ipairs({
                'Z',
                'X',
                'C',
                'V',
                'F',
            })

            while true do
                local u1079

                v1078, u1079 = v1076(v1077, v1078)

                if v1078 == nil then
                    break
                end

                wait()
                pcall(function()
                    u1068(u1079)
                end)
            end

            v1075.Parent = _Backpack

            break
        end
    end
end

v49.Sea:AddToggle('ToggleCollectFireFlowers', {
    Title = 'Auto Collect Fire Flowers',
    Description = '',
    Default = false,
}):OnChanged(function(p1087)
    _G.AutoCollectFireFlowers = p1087
end)
spawn(function()
    while wait() do
        local v1088 = _G.AutoCollectFireFlowers and workspace:FindFirstChild('FireFlowers')

        if v1088 then
            local v1089, v1090, v1091 = pairs(v1088:GetChildren())

            while true do
                local v1092

                v1091, v1092 = v1089(v1090, v1091)

                if v1091 == nil then
                    break
                end
                if v1092:IsA('Model') and v1092.PrimaryPart then
                    local _Position9 = v1092.PrimaryPart.Position

                    if (_Position9 - game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).Magnitude > 1 then
                        Tween2(CFrame.new(_Position9))
                    else
                        game:GetService('VirtualInputManager'):SendKeyEvent(true, 'E', false, game)
                        wait(1.5)

local function u1123()
    local _InteriorLava = game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core:FindFirstChild('InteriorLava')

    if _InteriorLava and _InteriorLava:IsA('Model') then
        _InteriorLava:Destroy()
    end

    local _PrehistoricIsland = game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')

    if _PrehistoricIsland then
        local v1110, v1111, v1112 = pairs(_PrehistoricIsland:GetDescendants())

        while true do
            local v1113

            v1112, v1113 = v1110(v1111, v1112)

            if v1112 == nil then
                break
            end
            if v1113:IsA('Part') and v1113.Name:lower():find('lava') then
                v1113:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end

    local _PrehistoricIsland2 = game.Workspace.Map:FindFirstChild('PrehistoricIsland')

    if _PrehistoricIsland2 then
        local v1115, v1116, v1117 = pairs(_PrehistoricIsland2:GetDescendants())

        while true do
            local v1118

            v1117, v1118 = v1115(v1116, v1117)

            if v1117 == nil then
                break
            end
            if v1118:IsA('Model') then
                local v1119, v1120, v1121 = pairs(v1118:GetDescendants())

                while true do
                    local v1122

                    v1121, v1122 = v1119(v1120, v1121)

                    if v1121 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1122:IsA('MeshPart') and v1122.Name:lower():find('lava') then
                        v1122:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
end
local function u1131()
    local _VolcanoRocks = game.Workspace.Map.PrehistoricIsland.Core.VolcanoRocks
    local v1125, v1126, v1127 = pairs(_VolcanoRocks:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v1128

        v1127, v1128 = v1125(v1126, v1127)

        if v1127 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v1128:IsA('Model') then
            local _volcanorock = v1128:FindFirstChild('volcanorock')

            if _volcanorock and _volcanorock:IsA('MeshPart') then
                local _Color = _volcanorock.Color

                if _Color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 56) or _Color == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 57) then
                    return _volcanorock
                end
            end
        end
    end

    return nil
end
local function u1143(p1132)
    local _LocalPlayer7 = game.Players.LocalPlayer
    local _Backpack2 = _LocalPlayer7.Backpack
    local v1135, v1136, v1137 = pairs(_Backpack2:GetChildren())

    while true do
        local v1138

        v1137, v1138 = v1135(v1136, v1137)

        if v1137 == nil then
            break
        end
        if v1138:IsA('Tool') and v1138.ToolTip == p1132 then
            v1138.Parent = _LocalPlayer7.Character

            local v1139, v1140, v1141 = ipairs({
                'Z',
                'X',
                'C',
                'V',
                'F',
            })

            while true do
                local u1142

                v1141, u1142 = v1139(v1140, v1141)

                if v1141 == nil then
                    break
                end

                wait()
                pcall(function()
                    u1107(u1142)
                end)
            end

            v1138.Parent = _Backpack2

            break
        end
    end
end

spawn(function()
    while wait() do
        if _G.AutoDefendVolcano then
            AutoHaki()
            pcall(u1123)

            local v1144 = u1131()

            if v1144 then
                local v1145 = CFrame.new(v1144.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))

                Tween2(v1145)

                local _Color2 = v1144.Color

                if _Color2 == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 56) or _Color2 == Color3.fromRGB(185, 53, 57) then
                    if (game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position - v1144.Position - Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)).Magnitude <= 1 then
                        if _G.UseMelee then
                            u1143('Melee')
                        end
                        if _G.UseSword then
                            u1143('Sword')
                        end
                        if _G.UseGun then
                            u1143('Gun')
                        end
                    end

                    _G.TweenToPrehistoric = false
                else
                    u1131()
                end
            else
                _G.TweenToPrehistoric = true
            end
        end
    end
end)

            pcall(function()

                while true do
                    local v1151

                    v1150, v1151 = v1148(v1149, v1150)

                    if v1150 == nil then
                        break
                    end
                    if v1151:FindFirstChild('Humanoid') and (v1151:FindFirstChild('HumanoidRootPart') and v1151.Humanoid.Health > 0) then
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            sethiddenproperty(game:GetService('Players').LocalPlayer, 'SimulationRadius', math.huge)

                            v1151.Humanoid.Health = 0
                            v1151.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
                        until not KillAura or (not v1151.Parent or v1151.Humanoid.Health <= 0)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end
